# Duplicate-Content Report — Wildlife Directory location pages

_Generated by `scripts/dup-content-detector.js`. Scope: authored content in the JSON data layer (`wildlife_intro`, `leadParagraph`, `intro`, `extendedBody`, and authored `faqs`). Each block is normalized: HTML stripped, whitespace collapsed, and the page's variable tokens (city, county, state, animal singular/plural) replaced with `loc` so name-swapped boilerplate collides._

## Summary

- **Total location pages scanned:** 1171
  - city-animal: 775
  - city-hub: 123
  - county-animal: 273
- **Pages with authored body content:** 1171 (0 have none of the scanned fields)
- **Distinct content blocks:** 20133
- **Distinct DUPLICATED blocks (appear on >1 page):** 1882
- **Block instances total:** 26168; instances that are duplicates: 7805 (29.8%)
- **Median page uniqueness:** 88.5%

## Top duplicated blocks (ranked by # of pages)

Each row: how many pages share the block, which field kind it came from, the first ~30 words (normalized tokens shown as `loc`), and a sample of affected URLs.
